17 “Went up in search of David” translated here as “nangipanew e sikandan langun eyew te pegdakep ki Dabid” because the purpose of the Philistines searching for David was to take him captive and thus reduce the power of the Israelites. Translators should therefore look for terms that convey this idea rather than using neutral terms that may leave the reader thinking that they merely wanted to meet a famous person(TH says).
